# Issue #764 C07 meaning audit

## Scope and evidence

This is the source-bound agent self-check for the C07 (ㅁ) Meaning shard, based on branch baseline `417aaf0fd718c6b1695b177b685348cdd6205729`. It re-judged every multi-Meaning lemma in the shard against all bound canonical STDICT senses and current Meaning rows. Canonical JSONL is unchanged. Singleton lemmas are not edited.

- C07 rows: 28572; lemmas: 21912; multi-Meaning lemmas reviewed: 3588; singleton lemmas left untouched: 18324.
- Current rows for reviewed lemmas: 10248; each currently contains one source sense.
- Final repartitions: 3588; source-bound merge groups: 2420; source-sense references in those groups: 5636. Every other source sense stays in its own existing Meaning with its current gloss.
- The current shard has 722 redirect Meaning rows. All are retained as standalone rows with their original ID, sense ID, and `→` gloss. 13 proposed groups had included redirect senses; those references were separated before finalizing the plan.

## Boundary decisions

The audit merges natural extensions of one lexical identity while keeping independent homonyms separate. It treats POS, concrete versus abstract use, figurative reference, register, and source record boundaries as evidence to assess rather than automatic split rules. Shared context, broad category resemblance, or an editor-invented umbrella alone does not establish a merge. Representative controls include the distinct horse, hemp, and unit senses under `마`; the separate speech, animal, game-piece, measure, and plant identities under `말`; distinct source meanings retained under `마치`; and separate animal/ritual identities under `매구`.

Representative broad groups were checked against every listed source gloss: `맞다` correctness/fit and its recollection interjection; `만들다` creation across objects, states, rules, resources and results; `막다` stopping/blocking and related settling/end uses; `말` speech-related extensions; `맞추다` alignment/correspondence; `밑` below/base extensions; `밀다` physical pushing and pressure/tool extensions; and `마디` as a joint or segment within a larger whole. Redirects remain standalone regardless of target similarity, including the malformed empty references in `마치`, `맹태`, and both `멍털군` senses.

Every final group was checked for exact per-lemma source coverage, unique membership, same-lemma canonical binding, and redirect isolation. The complete candidate-specific decision ledger follows.

## Candidate-specific decisions

- **ㅁ** — The letter name and its represented consonant are the same grapheme identity; merge character and sound uses. `w108012-s1 + w108012-s2` → “한글 자모 ‘ㅁ’을 이르거나, 그 자모가 나타내는 자음의 소리를 이른다.”
- **마** — Keep the horse chess piece, hemp plant, yard unit, sailor term for south, and musical note distinct. Merge the two surname records as the same Korean surname concept, the general/specific yam-plant descriptions, and the demonic hindrance/barrier/spirit uses as one lexical identity with figurative extensions. `w108946-s1 + w420023-s1` → “우리나라 성(姓)의 하나. 본관은 장흥(長興), 목천(木川), 영평(永平), 열산(烈山) 등이 현존한다.”; `w420022-s1 + w420022-s2` → “맛과의 덩굴풀을 통틀어 이르며, 특히 높이 1미터 정도로 자라고 실눈은 식용하고 뿌리는 약용하는 여러해살이풀을 말한다.”; `w420215-s1 + w420215-s2 + w420215-s3 + w420215-s4 + w420215-s5` → “일을 그르치게 하거나 넘기 어려운 장애물, 또는 궂은일이 잦은 장소나 때를 이르며, 사람을 홀려 악한 길로 유혹하는 귀신을 가리키기도 한다.”; separate senses: `w108087-s1`, `w108088-s1`, `w108089-s1`, `w108943-s1`, `w108944-s1`
- **마가** — The Baekje tribal office and the Christian figure Mark have unrelated referents; the redirect to 마하 is also separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107462-s1`, `w107463-s1`, `w107464-s1`
- **마각** — The horse-leg sense extends figuratively to the hidden nature or truth revealed behind a disguise. `w108216-s1 + w108216-s2` → “말의 다리를 이르며, 비유적으로는 가식 뒤에 숨은 본성이나 진상을 이른다.”
- **마감** — Merge finishing work and the end of its allotted period. Keep the redirect to 나중 and the separate historical Chinese official-rating system distinct. `w420016-s1 + w420016-s2` → “하던 일을 끝내거나 마무리하는 일, 또는 정해진 기한의 끝.”; separate senses: `w108955-s1`, `w108956-s1`
- **마감하다** — Finishing a task and examining officials to grade their performance are separate actions; the latter is tied to the historical rating sense of 마감. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108373-s1`, `w108957-s1`
- **마개** — A peeled hemp stalk and an object that plugs a bottle opening are unrelated referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108111-s1`, `w419032-s1`
- **마거리** — The redirect to 마수걸이 and the British diplomat are unrelated lexical identities. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107468-s1`, `w107469-s1`
- **마경** — Horse-plowing, a hemp stalk, and the world of demons have no source-bound lexical bridge. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108640-s1`, `w108641-s1`, `w108642-s1`
- **마계** — The horse-rental association and demon world are separate. The plant and its dried medicinal tuber are linked meanings of 삽주/백출 and form one plant-to-product extension. `w108645-s1 + w108645-s2` → “국화과의 여러해살이풀인 삽주를 이르며, 그 덩이줄기를 말려 만든 약재를 가리키기도 한다.”; separate senses: `w108644-s1`, `w108646-s1`
- **마곗말** — The first two senses are horses kept or leased by a 마계; the pejorative old-woman use extends figuratively from the old-horse use. `w417604-s1 + w417604-s2 + w417604-s3` → “마계에서 세로 주거나 기르던 말, 특히 늙은 말을 이르며, 비유적으로 교태를 부리는 늙은 여자를 낮잡아 이른다.”
- **마고할미** — The legendary immortal grandmother and the common old-woman use are a mythic figure and its ordinary referential extension. `w108440-s1 + w108440-s2` → “전설에 나오는 신선 할미를 이르며, 늙은 여자를 가리키기도 한다.”
- **마관** — A Joseon government office and another name for Shimonoseki are unrelated. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108594-s1`, `w108595-s1`
- **마광** — Both senses describe grinding material; milling ore and grinding jade, stone, or metal to polish it are material-specific uses of one action. `w108598-s1 + w419394-s1` → “광석을 잘게 부수거나 옥·돌·쇠붙이를 갈아 빛을 내는 일, 또는 그렇게 갈아 난 빛.”
- **마광하다** — The two verb senses share grinding material; only the material and purpose differ. `w108113-s1 + w108114-s1` → “광석을 잘게 부수거나 옥·돌·쇠붙이를 갈아 빛을 내다.”
- **마구** — Keep the baseball pitch, horse equipment, stable, and hemp/cord shoe apart. The two adverb senses share an excessive, uncontrolled manner and are grouped. `w420600-s1 + w420600-s2` → “정도를 넘을 만큼 몹시 세차게, 또는 아무렇게나 함부로.”; separate senses: `w107790-s1`, `w108118-s1`, `w108120-s1`, `w108121-s1`
- **마구리** — The end face of an elongated object and an attachment placed at its ends share the same end-of-object referent. `w106284-s1 + w106284-s2` → “길쭉한 물건의 양쪽 끝면이나 그 끝에 대는 것.”
- **마구마구** — Both adverb senses extend the same uncontrolled, excessive degree: forcefully/severely and without care or restraint. `w516140-s1 + w516140-s2` → “정도를 넘을 만큼 몹시 세차게, 또는 아무렇게나 함부로.”
- **마군** — The two historical cavalry descriptions are subdivisions of one mounted force; the demonic army and its figurative harmful acts share one Buddhist-demon image. `w107791-s1 + w107791-s2` → “조선 시대에 말을 타고 싸우던 군대. 금군·마병·기사나 별효사 등으로 이루어졌다.”; `w107792-s1 + w107792-s2` → “불도를 방해하는 마귀의 군사, 또는 그런 악한 일을 비유적으로 이르는 말.”
- **마굴** — The literal gathering place of demons extends metaphorically to places where wicked people gather. `w107793-s1 + w107793-s2` → “마귀들이 모여 있는 곳을 이르며, 못된 무리 등이 모인 곳을 비유적으로 이른다.”
- **마권** — A realm under demonic power and a horse-racing betting ticket have unrelated referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107795-s1`, `w423930-s1`
- **마귀** — Generic wicked spirits and evil personified as an adversary are two descriptions of the same demonic being. `w106661-s1 + w106661-s2` → “요사스럽고 못된 잡귀를 이르며, 하나님과 대립하는 악을 인격화한 존재를 가리키기도 한다.”
- **마기** — The redirect to 막상, Joseon horseback martial arts, and an ancient Persian priest are unrelated meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107066-s1`, `w107067-s1`, `w422876-s1`
- **마나** — A Melanesian supernatural force and the redirect to the biblical manna are unrelated. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w105954-s1`, `w108876-s1`
- **마냥** — Keep the late rice-transplanting noun apart. The adverb senses share unbounded extent in time or degree and are grouped. `w419600-s1 + w419600-s2 + w419600-s3` → “끝없이 계속하여, 부족함이 없도록 실컷, 또는 보통의 정도를 넘어 몹시.”; separate senses: `w105963-s1`
- **마녀** — The legendary witch extends metaphorically to a woman described as wicked like a demon. `w419601-s1 + w419601-s2` → “유럽 등의 전설에 나오는 요녀를 이르며, 비유적으로 악한 성질의 여자를 이른다.”
- **마녀사냥** — The historical witch persecution and the figurative framing of a person as guilty are literal and figurative uses of the same accusation concept. `w107332-s1 + w107332-s2` → “마녀로 판결해 처형하던 역사적 일이나, 비유적으로 특정 사람에게 죄를 뒤집어씌우는 일을 이른다.”
- **마녀재판** — The historical witch trial and figurative assignment of guilt are literal and figurative uses; canonical direct links align both senses with 마녀사냥. `w107536-s1 + w107536-s2` → “마녀로 판결해 처형하던 역사적 재판이나, 비유적으로 특정 사람에게 죄를 뒤집어씌우는 일을 이른다.”
- **마누라** — The familiar term for a middle-aged wife extends colloquially and disparagingly to a middle-aged woman. `w418819-s1 + w418819-s2` → “중년이 넘은 아내를 허물없이, 또는 중년이 넘은 여자를 속되게 이르는 말.”
- **마늘모** — The radical name, triangular garlic-clove shape, and diagonal Go move are connected by the same named shape. `w106483-s1 + w418626-s1 + w418626-s2` → “한자 부수 ‘厶’의 이름이며, 마늘쪽처럼 세모진 모양이나 그 모양으로 바둑돌을 놓는 수를 이른다.”
- **마늘쪽** — A single garlic clove and several individual cloves are quantity variants of the same named unit. `w106552-s1 + w106552-s2` → “마늘의 낱개를 이르며, 낱낱의 마늘 몇 개를 가리키기도 한다.”
- **마니** — A Buddhist jewel and Mani, the founder of Manichaeism, are unrelated referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w106557-s1`, `w106558-s1`
- **마님** — The honorific applies to a high-status woman of the household or to the household master; gender/role alone does not create a separate lexical identity. `w420493-s1 + w420493-s2` → “지체 높은 집안의 부인이나 상전(上典)을 높여 이르는 말.”
- **마다** — The verb meaning to crush and the noun naming a Brahmi vowel are unrelated. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107182-s1`, `w107185-s1`
- **마당** — The physical yard extends to the place, field/situation, and performance-unit uses; all preserve the yard/place-of-action core. `w420031-s1 + w420031-s2 + w420031-s3 + w420031-s4` → “집 앞뒤의 평평한 땅이나 일이 이루어지는 장소·판·상황을 이르며, 판소리나 탈춤 단락의 단위로도 쓴다.”
- **마당놀이** — The general folk performance held in a yard and a particular scene in a mask performance are category and instance uses. `w107319-s1 + w107319-s2` → “마당에서 하는 민속놀이, 특히 북청 사자놀음의 한 장면을 이른다.”
- **마당발** — The socially connected person is a figurative extension of the broad, flat foot image. `w420032-s1 + w420032-s2` → “볼이 넓고 바닥이 평평한 발을 이르며, 비유적으로 인간관계가 넓어 폭넓게 활동하는 사람을 이른다.”
- **마당삼** — Both senses name ginseng by where/how it grows; clustered growth and cultivation in a house yard are location variants, not separate homonyms. `w107290-s1 + w107290-s2` → “한곳에 뭉쳐 나거나 집 마당에서 기르는 삼.”
- **마당쇠** — The old servant and the named Ogwangdae character/masked performer are one folk-character role and its performer. `w420033-s1 + w420033-s2` → “예전의 머슴이나 종을 이르며, 가산 오광대의 여섯 번째 과장에 나오는 탈 또는 그 탈을 쓰고 춤추는 사람을 이른다.”
- **마대** — A mountain gatherers’ word for walking stick, mounted troops, furniture support legs, and a coarse-hemp sack have unrelated referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w106923-s1`, `w106924-s1`, `w106925-s1`, `w422891-s1`
- **마도** — Keep the shellfish separate. The wrong-path and demonic-world senses share the evil/spiritual domain and are grouped. `w107443-s1 + w107443-s2` → “옳지 못한 길이나 악마의 세계를 이른다.”; separate senses: `w107442-s1`
- **마돈나** — The honorific for a noble lady or beloved woman extends to the specific revered woman, the Virgin Mary. `w418851-s1 + w418851-s2` → “귀부인이나 애인을 높여 부르는 말이며, 성모 마리아를 달리 이르기도 한다.”
- **마두** — A person responsible for postal horses and a jailer in hell have unrelated roles. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w419020-s1`, `w419020-s2`
- **마두라** — The Indonesian island and the city in Tamil Nadu are different places with the same transliterated name. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107374-s1`, `w107374-s2`
- **마들가리** — The branchless trunk and small wood pieces for fuel share the wood/stem use. The worn garment seam and a knot in thread/straw share the textile/fiber joining use; the wood and textile meanings remain distinct. `w106464-s1 + w106464-s2` → “나무의 가지 없는 줄기나, 잔가지·줄거리 토막으로 된 땔나무를 이른다.”; `w106464-s3 + w106464-s4` → “해어진 옷의 솔기나 새끼·실 따위가 훑이어 맺힌 마디를 이른다.”
- **마등갱이** — The two source redirects target different headwords; preserve each original redirect and source ID as a separate Meaning. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w106470-s1`, `w106470-s2`
- **마디** — Keep the Muslim savior (Mahdi) separate. The other eight source senses describe a joint, knot, segment, node, grammatical unit, or measure within a larger continuous whole. `w418821-s1 + w418821-s2 + w418821-s3 + w418821-s4 + w418821-s5 + w418821-s6 + w418821-s7 + w418821-s8` → “대·뼈·곤충 몸의 연결 부분이나 실이 맺힌 곳, 말·글·노래·악보의 한 토막 또는 파동의 마디를 이른다.”; separate senses: `w106705-s1`
- **마디다** — Wear/consumption that proceeds slowly and growth that proceeds slowly share the same slow rate or durability meaning. `w418805-s1 + w418805-s2` → “쉽게 닳거나 없어지지 않으며, 자라는 속도가 더디다.”
- **마디마디** — All four distributive senses name each joint or segment throughout a continuous body, thread, text, or song. `w106013-s1 + w106013-s2 + w106013-s3 + w106013-s4` → “대·나무·뼈·곤충 몸의 마디, 실이 맺힌 부분, 말·글·노래의 토막을 낱낱이 이른다.”
- **마디에 옹이** — Both idiom senses describe difficulties or obstacles piling up by coincidence; the canonical direct links align them with the same two meanings of 기침에 재채기. `w527270-s1 + w527270-s2` → “어려운 일이나 방해가 공교롭게 겹쳐 일이 순조롭게 풀리지 않음을 비유적으로 이르는 말.”
- **마디충** — The broad stem-boring pest category includes the specifically described rice stem-borer larva. `w420663-s1 + w420663-s2` → “식물 줄기 속을 파먹는 벌레를 통틀어 이르며, 특히 벼·조·피 따위를 말라 죽게 하는 이화명나방의 애벌레를 가리킨다.”
- **마디충나방** — The family-wide category and the specifically described moth are a broad class and its named member. `w106759-s1 + w106759-s2` → “명나방과 곤충을 통틀어 이르며, 특히 몸길이 1~1.2cm인 회색빛 갈색 나방을 가리킨다.”
- **마디충나비** — The family-wide category and the specifically described moth are a broad class and its named member. `w108911-s1 + w108911-s2` → “명나방과 곤충을 통틀어 이르며, 특히 몸길이 1~1.2cm인 회색빛 갈색 나방을 가리킨다.”
- **마땅하다** — Being suitable, satisfying, or right follows one evaluative notion of fittingness; the contexts differ without establishing homonyms. `w420456-s1 + w420456-s2 + w420456-s3` → “일정한 조건에 알맞거나 흡족하고, 이치로 보아 그렇게 하는 것이 옳다.”
- **마땅히** — The adverb uses preserve the same fittingness/evaluative axis: appropriately, satisfactorily, or rightly. `w108931-s1 + w108931-s2 + w108931-s3` → “일정한 조건에 알맞게 또는 흡족하게, 이치로 보아 옳게.”
- **마라** — The demon and French revolutionary Jean-Paul Marat are unrelated referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108990-s1`, `w418310-s1`
- **마레** — The French physiologist and German painter are different people with a shared transliterated name. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108041-s1`, `w419228-s1`
- **마력** — The supernatural power that fascinates and the technical horsepower unit have distinct roots and meanings despite both involving power. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108045-s1`, `w419230-s1`
- **마련** — The preparation/planning noun forms one meaning. The fixed grammatical uses describing expected occurrence or a resulting degree/state form one grammaticalized use. `w417207-s1 + w417207-s2` → “헤아려 갖추는 일이나 어떤 일을 하기 위한 속셈·궁리를 이른다.”; `w417207-s3 + w417207-s4` → “당연히 그러할 것임이나 어떤 정도·상태에 이름을 나타내는 말.”
- **마련하다** — Preparing or arranging something and the bound comparative sense (“그런 것치고는/그런 것에 비하여서는”) carry different source-bound content; the grammatical form alone is not used as the split reason. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107985-s1`, `w107985-s2`
- **마령** — A bell hung on a horse and a humble expression for a person’s age based on horse years are distinct meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107422-s1`, `w107423-s1`
- **마료** — A named Polygonaceae plant and the general fodder or grain used to feed horses have different source definitions; the source does not bind the plant specifically to that fodder category. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107631-s1`, `w419067-s1`
- **마루** — The indoor plank floor remains separate. - **만과** — A large recruitment exam, a late exam, the old evening prayer term, and deception are separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108562-s1`, `w108679-s1`, `w108680-s1`, `w420646-s1`
- **만관** — Reaching the maximum mahjong score and the payment/item awarded at that score are the score state and its payoff. `w420471-s1 + w420471-s2` → “마작에서 최고 한도의 점수에 이르거나, 만관이 되었을 때 받는 약을 이른다.”; separate senses: `w108620-s1`
- **만구** — A bay inlet and horse harness are separate. Many people’s mouths/words and a metaphor for many people are the collective-mouth extension. `w420473-s1 + w420473-s2` → “많은 사람의 입이나 말을 이르며, 비유적으로 많은 사람을 가리킨다.”; separate senses: `w107946-s1`, `w109019-s1`
- **만국어** — A language understood in all countries and the set of languages spoken around the world are different meanings: shared universal language vs all national languages. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w515361-s1`, `w515361-s2`
- **만군** — Separate the homonymous Manchukuo army abbreviation and the archaic “barbarian army” term. The “many soldiers” sense extends to the total Israelite host and all beings in the universe; those three are one lexical identity. `w420224-s1 + w420224-s2 + w420224-s3` → “많은 군사나 그에 비유하여 이스라엘 민족 전체 또는 우주에 존재하는 모든 것을 이르는 말.”; separate senses: `w108822-s1`, `w108833-s1`
- **만근** — Keep the heavy-weight term, uninterrupted attendance for a period, and a span from several years ago through the present distinct; their source meanings do not express one lexical core. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w107518-s1`, `w107519-s1`, `w107521-s1`
- **만기** — Within this lemma, the late-life period is distinct from expiration and from the unrelated musical tempo. Its source gloss points to 만년(晩年), whose target sense w418350-s1 belongs to a different lemma and therefore cannot be merged here. Expiration, the elapsed term, and a bill payment date share the end-of-term identity. The political 万機 senses describe the ruler’s many important affairs and related secrets as one identity; the multitude of cavalry is separate, with the two cavalry glosses grouped together. `w107524-s2 + w420197-s1 + w420197-s2` → “정해진 기간이 끝나는 때나 그 기한이며, 특히 어음 금액의 지급일로 적힌 날짜.”; `w107525-s1 + w107525-s2 + w107525-s3` → “정치에서 임금이 맡는 온갖 중요한 정무와 그에 딸린 기밀.”; `w420196-s1 + w420196-s2` → “많은 기병 또는 그 군세.”; separate senses: `w107524-s1`, `w107526-s1`
- **만기일** — A date on which a term ends and the date written for payment of a bill are one expiration-date identity. `w108801-s1 + w108801-s2` → “정해진 기간이 끝나는 날이며, 특히 어음 금액의 지급일로 적힌 날짜.”
- **만끽** — Eating and drinking to one’s heart’s content and fully satisfying a desire are the concrete and broader uses of the same “enjoy/indulge fully” identity. `w419213-s1 + w419213-s2` → “마음껏 먹고 마시거나 욕망을 충분히 충족함.”
- **만끽하다** — The eating/drinking use and the broader desire-satisfaction use share the same “enjoy/indulge fully” lexical identity. `w108806-s1 + w108806-s2` → “마음껏 먹고 마시거나 욕망을 충분히 충족하다.”
- **만나** — Keep the biblical miraculous food distinct from the plant-derived sweet laxative substance. The committed source glosses identify different referents and do not establish a shared lexical extension; the shared written name alone is insufficient to merge them. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108666-s1`, `w419235-s1`
- **만나다** — All seven senses develop from encountering or coming into contact with a person, object, event, time, or circumstance: physical intersection, face-to-face meeting, confronting a fact, undergoing an event or time, meeting weather, and forming a relationship by fate are extensions of one lexical identity. `w419253-s1 + w419253-s2 + w419253-s3 + w419253-s4 + w419253-s5 + w419253-s6 + w419253-s7` → “사람이나 사물, 선 따위와 마주 닿거나 마주하며, 어떤 일이나 때를 겪거나 인연을 맺다.”
- **만난각** — The angle made by two lines and the angle formed by tangents at an intersection of curves or circles are the same geometric intersection-angle identity. `w108669-s1 + w108669-s2` → “두 직선이 만나 이루는 각 또는 교점에서 접선이 이루는 각.”
- **만년** — “A long time” and “an always unchanged state” are durative and persistent uses of one identity. The late-life period belongs to the separate 晩年 identity and remains distinct. `w107363-s1 + w107363-s2` → “오랜 세월 동안 변함없이 이어지는 상태.”; separate senses: `w418350-s1`
- **만년빙** — Permanent ice and snow that remains in high or cold regions and gradually becomes ice are stages or forms of the same persistent glacial mass, not independent homonyms. `w108566-s1 + w108566-s2` → “추운 지방이나 높은 산지에서 녹지 않고 남아 얼음으로 변해 가는 눈이나 얼음.”
- **만년송** — Keep the Selaginella plant and the pine-family evergreen tree distinct: the source descriptions identify different botanical referents, and a shared plant name does not establish one lexical identity. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w108569-s1`, `w108569-s2`
- **만년필** — The fountain pen and a long pen-shaped object are literal and figurative uses of the same pen identity. `w423547-s1 + w423547-s2` → “잉크를 펜촉으로 흘려 글씨를 쓰는 펜 또는 그 모양을 닮은 기다란 물건.”
- **만능선수** — A player skilled across all competitions, across every aspect of a game, and a person capable in every kind of work are contextual extensions of one all-rounder identity. `w109971-s1 + w109971-s2 + w109971-s3` → “경기의 모든 분야나 여러 일을 두루 잘하는 선수 또는 사람.”
- **만다라** — The Buddhist diagram representing the virtues of the dharma realm and the ritual dais where Buddhas or bodhisattvas are enshrined and worshipped belong to the same mandala ritual concept. `w425870-s1 + w425870-s2` → “불교에서 부처나 보살을 모시고 예배·공양하는 단 또는 우주 법계의 덕을 나타내는 그림.”
- **만다라화** — The lotus blossom and sacred heavenly white lotus are the same floral identity in ordinary and Buddhist/mythic use. The two botanically distinct wild plants remain separate homonyms. `w110221-s1 + w110221-s2` → “연꽃 또는 천상계에 핀다고 하는 성스러운 흰 연꽃.”; separate senses: `w110221-s3`, `w110221-s4`
- **만단** — The Hanafuda use for all kinds of scoring yaku and the senses for many tangled strands or all kinds share the same “many/various kinds” identity. `w110223-s1 + w425842-s1 + w425842-s2` → “여러 갈래나 종류가 아주 많음.”
- **만달** — A vine-entanglement motif in East Asian painting and a rise in status and reputation in old age have unrelated referents and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110291-s1`, `w110292-s1`
- **만답** — A humorous, banter-like answer and a casual, thoughtless answer are styles of the same flippant responding identity, rather than independent homonyms. `w109470-s1 + w109470-s2` → “익살스럽거나 별 생각 없이 함부로 대답함. 또는 그런 대답.”
- **만답하다** — The humorous and thoughtless answer senses share the same casual/flippant answering identity in verb form. `w109471-s1 + w109471-s2` → “익살스럽거나 별 생각 없이 함부로 대답하다.”
- **만당** — A room or hall full of people and the state of being filled with people are the same crowd/fullness identity. The late-Tang literary period is a separate homonym. `w109510-s1 + w109510-s2` → “사람이나 사람이 있는 곳이 가득 참. 또는 그렇게 가득한 사람들.”; separate senses: `w419998-s1`
- **만도** — Late arrival, late-ripening rice, an evening prayer, and the whole city have unrelated temporal, agricultural, religious, and collective referents; keep them separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110418-s1`, `w110649-s1`, `w110650-s1`, `w110651-s1`
- **만동** — Late winter and a child of the Man/Bárbarian people are unrelated identities and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110658-s1`, `w422804-s1`
- **만두** — The filled flour food and the edge of a bay are homonyms with different referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110486-s1`, `w110487-s1`
- **만득** — The act of bearing a child in old age and the child born that way are an event/result pair within one identity. `w110420-s1 + w110420-s2` → “늙어서 자식을 낳음. 또는 그렇게 낳은 자식.”
- **만들다** — All source senses extend the same productive verb of bringing something into being: objects and works, states and conditions, rules and groups, resources, time, injuries, trouble, media, and results. They do not establish independent homonyms. `w420130-s1 + w420130-s2 + w420130-s3 + w420130-s4 + w420130-s5 + w420130-s6 + w420130-s7 + w420130-s8 + w420130-s9 + w420130-s10 + w420130-s11 + w420130-s12 + w420130-s13` → “어떤 사물이나 상태·일을 새로 이루거나 생기게 하다.”
- **만등** — The many lamps and the Buddhist ceremony of lighting ten thousand lamps are the literal and event/metonymic uses of one lamp-offering identity. `w110424-s1 + w110424-s2` → “수많은 등불 또는 참회와 공양을 위해 만 개의 등불을 켜는 불교 법회.”
- **만량** — The cool air around evening and the cool evening time are the atmospheric condition and time-of-day uses of one identity. `w110642-s1 + w110642-s2` → “저녁 무렵의 서늘한 기운이나 서늘해진 저녁때.”
- **만려** — A specific plowing tool, beautiful skin, and considering something in many ways have unrelated referents and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109723-s1`, `w109724-s1`, `w111116-s1`
- **만려하다** — The adjective meaning that the skin is beautiful and the verb meaning to consider in many ways are separate homonyms with different parts of speech and meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109725-s1`, `w111117-s1`
- **만력** — Keep the century-length astronomical/calendar book, the Ming dynasty reign name, and indiscriminate force or courage separate; their source glosses identify unrelated objects and concepts. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111119-s1`, `w111120-s1`, `w111121-s1`
- **만록** — The green appearance/green forest of summer and an informal, impression-driven essay have unrelated meanings and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111124-s1`, `w111125-s1`
- **만뢰** — The sounds produced by all of nature and numerous thunderclaps or a thunder-like roar are separately lexicalized sound concepts; the shared loud/sound field alone does not establish one identity. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111126-s1`, `w420119-s1`
- **만류** — All things in the world, holding someone back by persuasion, and the ocean current around a gulf are unrelated lexical identities. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111144-s1`, `w423722-s1`, `w423723-s1`
- **만리장성** — The literal Chinese Great Wall anchors all three figurative uses: an impassable barrier, a flourishing future, and sexual union. - **만만** — The exact number one hundred million and the noun, determiner, and adverbial uses for an immense degree or totality share the hyperbolic 萬萬 identity. `w110055-s1 + w110055-s2 + w110055-s3 + w110055-s4 + w110055-s5` → “만의 만 배인 1억 또는 그처럼 정도가 매우 크거나 완전함을 나타내는 말.”
- **만만찮다** — The senses “not easy to handle,” “not easy,” “substantial in quantity,” and “uncomfortable” express the same nontrivial, difficult-to-dismiss state in the negative form of 만만하다. `w425883-s1 + w425883-s2 + w425883-s3 + w425883-s4` → “쉽게 다루거나 지나치기 어렵고, 양이 적지 않거나 편안하지 않다.”
- **만만하다** — Softness/pliability extends to being easy to approach or handle, so those two senses share one identity. Sufficiency/fullness and endless tediousness are distinct homonyms. The National Institute of Korean Language also lists 滿滿하다 and 漫漫하다 as separate homonymous entries. `w109533-s1 + w109533-s2` → “연하고 보드라워 쉽게 다루거나 대할 만하다.”; separate senses: `w110004-s1`, `w110006-s1`
- **만만히** — The soft/pliable manner and the manner of being easy to deal with form one adverbial identity. The abundant/full and endless/tedious adverbs remain separate homonyms. `w110054-s1 + w110054-s2` → “연하고 보드랍게 또는 쉽게 다루거나 대할 만하게.”; separate senses: `w110005-s1`, `w110007-s1`
- **만망** — A view at sunset and sincerely wishing for something have unrelated meanings and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109535-s1`, `w109536-s1`
- **만명** — The historical human Manmyeong, Kim Yusin’s mother, and the shaman deity formed by deifying her are the same person/identity. `w109268-s1 + w109268-s2` → “신라 장군 김유신의 어머니 만명 부인 또는 그를 신격화하여 모시는 무속신.”
- **만모** — Sunset and the late stage of a person’s life are the evening/old-age extensions of one “late twilight” identity. The arrogant, contemptuous attitude is a separate homonym. `w425641-s1 + w425641-s2` → “해가 질 무렵이나 비유적으로 나이가 들어 늙어 가는 시기.”; separate senses: `w109269-s1`
- **만목** — The vine-growing tree and many people’s eyes are separate homonyms. “Filling the eye” and the limit of what can be seen belong to the same visual-field identity. `w425642-s1 + w425642-s2` → “눈에 가득 차 보이는 모습이나 눈에 보이는 데까지의 한계.”; separate senses: `w108353-s1`, `w109271-s1`
- **만무방** — The eponymous Kim Yujeong short story is a distinct named work. The shameless/ragged person and the person described as carelessly or ill-favoredly formed are closely related derogatory uses of one identity. `w422841-s1 + w422841-s2` → “염치없이 막되거나 아무렇게나 생긴 사람.”; separate senses: `w109412-s1`
- **만무일실** — No risk of failure or mistake and no loss whatsoever express one assurance of complete, unerring preservation. `w109655-s1 + w109655-s2` → “실패·실수하거나 조금이라도 축날 염려가 없음.”
- **만무일실하다** — The no-failure and no-loss readings are the same complete, unerring state in adjective form. `w422843-s1 + w422843-s2` → “실패·실수하거나 조금이라도 축날 염려가 없다.”
- **만문** — The two free-form, impression-driven prose senses describe one essay/writing identity. The Manchu phonemic script is separate. The humorous and thoughtless question senses are styles of one flippant asking identity. `w109656-s1 + w109656-s2` → “일정한 형식 없이 생각나는 대로 쓰는 산문이나 글.”; `w422860-s1 + w422860-s2` → “만담처럼 익살스럽거나 생각나는 대로 함부로 물음. 또는 그런 물음.”; separate senses: `w109657-s1`
- **만문하다** — Humorous banter and asking without much thought are styles of the same casual/flippant questioning identity. Preserve the separate source redirect to 만만하다01 as its own bound source sense. `w109658-s1 + w109658-s2` → “만담처럼 익살스럽거나 생각나는 대로 함부로 묻다.”; separate senses: `w109659-s1`
- **만물** — The last weeding of a rice paddy and all things in the world are unrelated agricultural and universal-collective identities. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109660-s1`, `w422862-s1`
- **만물상** — The shop that sells everyday goods and the merchant who operates it are metonymic roles within one general-goods-store identity. The variety of forms of all things and the named Geumgangsan rock mountain are separate senses. `w109663-s1 + w109663-s2` → “일상생활에 필요한 온갖 물건을 파는 장사나 그 가게·장수.”; separate senses: `w422863-s1`, `w422863-s2`
- **만발** — Ten thousand offering bowls, flowers in full bloom, and the obsolete term for delay are unrelated identities and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109299-s1`, `w109300-s1`, `w422834-s1`
- **만발하다** — Flowering fully and figuratively having conjectures or laughter arise in profusion are literal/figurative extensions of one blooming/proliferating identity. The old-term redirect to 지연하다 remains separate. `w422835-s1 + w422835-s2` → “꽃이 활짝 피거나 추측·웃음 따위가 한꺼번에 많이 일어나다.”; separate senses: `w109298-s1`
- **만방** — Everywhere, the many places where attention and effort are directed, and all countries of the world express an all-regions identity. The Go scoring threshold is a separate technical meaning. `w109357-s1 + w109357-s2 + w109358-s1` → “모든 곳이나 세계의 여러 나라, 마음과 힘이 미치는 여러 군데.”; separate senses: `w109359-s1`
- **만범순풍** — A favorable wind filling a sail and the figurative state of affairs going as intended are a direct image-based extension. `w108198-s1 + w108198-s2` → “돛을 불룩하게 하는 순풍 또는 일이 뜻대로 잘되어 가는 형편.”
- **만범하다** — A sail filled with wind and work proceeding as intended are literal and figurative extensions of one identity. `w109823-s1 + w109823-s2` → “돛에 바람이 차서 가득하거나 일이 뜻대로 잘되어 가다.”
- **만법** — All legal rules and all tangible or intangible phenomena in the universe are the rule/phenomenon totality of one 万法 identity. `w108199-s1 + w108199-s2` → “모든 법률·규칙 또는 우주에 있는 유형·무형의 모든 사물.”
- **만변** — Endless change and the occurrence of many kinds of upheaval are one identity of manifold change and its adverse events. `w422619-s1 + w422619-s2` → “끝없이 변화하거나 여러 가지 변고가 있음.”
- **만변하다** — Endless change and various upheavals are the same manifold-change identity in verb form. `w108204-s1 + w108204-s2` → “끝없이 변화하거나 여러 가지 변고가 있다.”
- **만병통치** — A prescription curing every illness and a measure that works across many kinds of situations are the literal and figurative extensions of a universal remedy. `w109235-s1 + w109235-s2` → “온갖 병을 다 고치는 처방 또는 여러 경우에 두루 효력을 내는 대책.”
- **만병통치약** — A medicine for all diseases and a figurative all-purpose measure are literal and figurative uses of one universal-remedy identity. `w109275-s1 + w109275-s2` → “온갖 병을 고치는 약이나 처방 또는 여러 경우에 두루 효력을 내는 대책.”
- **만병통치하다** — Curing every illness and figuratively making one measure work in many circumstances are the literal and figurative uses of one universal-remedy identity. `w109236-s1 + w109236-s2` → “한 가지 처방으로 온갖 병을 고치거나, 한 가지 대책이 여러 경우에 두루 효력을 내다.”
- **만보** — Various treasures, leisurely walking, a deceptive report, and a labor-payment ticket have unrelated referents and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109276-s1`, `w109277-s1`, `w109279-s1`, `w422622-s1`
- **만보하다** — Leisurely walking and reporting falsely are unrelated actions and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109278-s1`, `w422623-s1`
- **만복** — Late-life fortune, all kinds of blessings, and a full stomach are separate identities despite the shared 만복 spelling. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109713-s1`, `w109714-s1`, `w109825-s1`
- **만복하다** — Being very blessed and having a full stomach have unrelated predicates and remain separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109824-s1`, `w422777-s1`
- **만부** — Numerous men or able-bodied workers are a specific instance of the broader “many people” sense, so both belong to one collective identity. `w109828-s1 + w109828-s2` → “수많은 사람, 특히 사내나 장정.”

- **만사하다** — Repeated gratitude and repeated apology are styles of the same formal act of repeated thanks/contrition. `w423518-s1 + w423518-s2` → “거듭거듭 감사하거나 사죄하다.”

- **만산편야** — Filling mountains and fields and figuratively describing a great many people are literal and figurative uses of one abundance image. `w110506-s1 + w110506-s2` → “산과 들에 가득하거나, 비유적으로 사람이 매우 많음.”
- **만산하다** — Ending a Buddhist assembly, bearing a child in old age, and filling the whole mountain are separate identities and remain distinct. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111302-s1`, `w423110-s1`, `w423111-s1`
- **만상** — Late-spring frost, all auspicious events, and a merchant from Yongman are separate identities. “All kinds of appearances” and “the forms of all things” describe the same manifold-phenomena identity. `w110514-s1 + w110516-s1` → “온갖 모양이나 사물의 형상.”; separate senses: `w110513-s1`, `w110515-s1`, `w425854-s1`
- **만색** — A view at sunset and scenery in the late season share the late-time/late-season landscape identity. `w110836-s1 + w110836-s2` → “해 질 무렵이나 철이 늦은 때의 경치.”
- **만생** — Growing with a vine stem is separate from the late-childbirth identity. Bearing a child in old age and the humble first-person “late-born” use share the late-birth/younger-generation core. `w111031-s1 + w111031-s2` → “늙어서 자식을 낳음 또는 선배에게 자신을 낮추어 이르는 말.”; separate senses: `w110521-s1`

- **만석** — Ten thousand seom of grain and a very large amount of grain are the exact and generalized quantities of one abundant-grain identity. A venue filled in every seat is separate. `w110234-s1 + w110234-s2` → “곡식 일만 섬 또는 그처럼 아주 많은 곡식.”; separate senses: `w534514-s1`
- **만선** — The cicada calling in the evening and the cicada calling late in the season are timing variants of one late-singing identity. The “all good deeds” sense and the named play are separate. - **만성** — All surnames and all people form one collective identity. A vine-growing habit, delayed formation, and uncultivated/low-cultural character are separate. - **망구다** — Causing ruin and destroying something until unusable are degrees of the same destructive result. `w111799-s1 + w111799-s2` → “망하게 하거나 파괴하여 못 쓰게 하다.”

- **망그러지다** — Physical breakage extends figuratively to a situation or condition becoming bad. `w423291-s1 + w423291-s2` → “부서지거나 찌그러져 못 쓰게 되거나, 상황이나 상태가 나빠지다.”
- **망극하다** — Boundless parental or royal grace and profound grief at misfortune to a parent or sovereign are distinct emotional meanings; the shared intensity alone does not establish one identity. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111223-s1`, `w423292-s1`
- **망기** — The flag hung on a watchtower and the flag used there to signal scouts are one object with its function. Forgetting a fact extends to forgetting worldly concerns and desires. - **망기하다** — Forgetting, performing the distant-family mourning rite, and reading a sign to infer an omen are unrelated source meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111921-s1`, `w111924-s1`, `w111925-s1`
- **망나니** — The insulting term for a violently disorderly person plausibly extends from the historical executioner role; the millipede is a distinct animal name. `w423071-s1 + w423071-s2` → “언동이 몹시 막된 사람을 비난조로 이르거나, 예전에 사형을 집행하던 사람.”; separate senses: `w423071-s3`
- **망녀** — A deceased daughter and a very disorderly woman are unrelated homonymous senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w109430-s1`, `w109430-s2`
- **망년** — Forgetting the year’s troubles and disregarding age differences both express setting aside a particular time-related concern. `w109431-s1 + w109431-s2` → “그해의 괴로움이나 나이의 차이를 잊음.”

- **망단** — The childbirth-related term, rash judgment, failure of a hoped-for matter, and resulting predicament are not safely reducible to a single identity. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110329-s1`, `w110330-s1`, `w110331-s1`, `w110331-s2`

- **망덕** — A harmful deed that ruins oneself or one’s family and the Christian theological virtue of hope are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110264-s1`, `w110265-s1`

- **망령** — Senile or abnormal conduct and the spirit of a deceased person are unrelated homonyms. The spirit meaning extends figuratively to a hated remnant of the past. `w111181-s1 + w111181-s2` → “죽은 사람의 영혼이나 혐오스러운 과거의 잔재.”; separate senses: `w110380-s1`

- **망막** — The eye’s light-sensitive retina and the serous lining of body cavities are distinct anatomical tissues; sharing the word membrane is insufficient to merge them. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110746-s1`, `w110746-s2`
- **망막하다** — Being broad and distant and lacking clear distinction are related uses of a hazy, indistinct expanse. `w419941-s1 + w419941-s2` → “넓고 멀거나 뚜렷한 구별이 없이 흐릿하다.”
- **망망연하다** — Looking into a far distance and the shy, sidelong gaze describe distinct states and are kept separate. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110829-s1`, `w110829-s2`
- **망망연히** — The adverbial senses parallel 망망연하다 and describe distinct distant-looking and shy-looking manners. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w110830-s1`, `w110830-s2`
- **망망하다** — The busy sense is distinct. Within w110825, physical breadth/distance and a hazy, far-off appearance form one connected spatial-perceptual identity. `w110825-s1 + w110825-s2` → “넓고 멀거나 어렴풋하고 아득하다.”; separate senses: `w110823-s1`

- **망명** — Political refuge and flight by a person guilty of a capital crime share the core act of leaving one’s country to escape danger or punishment; the motive differs. `w110991-s1 + w110991-s2` → “박해나 처벌을 피하여 외국으로 몸을 옮기거나 멀리 도망함.”
- **망명 정부** — The Korean provisional government in China is a specific historical instance of a government formed abroad by exiled political figures. `w427266-s1 + w427266-s2` → “정복·전쟁·혁명 등으로 외국에 피신한 정객들이 세운 정부, 특히 중국에 세웠던 대한민국 임시 정부.”

- **망모** — A deceased mother and deception or a deceptive scheme are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111270-s1`, `w111271-s1`
- **망물** — The two insulting descriptions both refer broadly to a contemptible or unreasonable person; the deceased monk’s relic is a separate homonym. `w110563-s1 + w423715-s1` → “아주 몹쓸 짓을 하거나 도리에 어긋나는 사람.”; separate senses: `w110564-s1`
- **망발** — A mistaken or irrational utterance/action and conduct that dishonors oneself or one’s ancestors are the same kind of improper speech or behavior, with the latter stating its consequence. `w423505-s1 + w423505-s2` → “망령이나 실수로 그릇된 말이나 행동을 하여 자신이나 조상을 욕되게 함.”
- **망발되다** — Improper speech/action and the resulting dishonor to oneself or ancestors are one event described by its act and consequence. `w110626-s1 + w110626-s2` → “망령이나 실수로 말이나 행동을 잘못하여 자신이나 조상이 욕되게 되다.”

- **망석** — A pair of stone pillars before a grave and a woven straw mat are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w111829-s1`, `w112058-s1`

- **망양지탄** — The lament over many divergent paths in learning and the lament upon seeing an immense sea are separate allusive idioms with different occasions. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w112190-s1`, `w112191-s1`

- **망우** — Forgetting worries and a drink named as something that makes all sorrows forgotten are one direct and metaphorical identity; the dead friend and aquatic plant are unrelated. `w426847-s1 + w426847-s2` → “근심을 잊거나 온갖 시름을 잊게 하는 술.”; separate senses: `w111602-s1`, `w111603-s1`

- **머리(를) 모으다** — People physically gathering to talk and pooling their views are a concrete and figurative use of bringing heads together. `w527507-s1 + w527507-s2` → “이야기하거나 의견을 모으기 위해 여러 사람이 가까이 모이다.”
- **머리(를) 숙이다** — Submission, admiration, agreement, and respect are attitudes expressed by the same act of lowering one’s head. `w527508-s1 + w527508-s2` → “굴복하거나 탄복·수긍·존경하여 머리를 낮추다.”
- **머리(를) 얹다** — Arranging long hair, the traditional hair arrangement marking a gisaeng’s formal debut, and a woman’s marriage are related hair-setting uses of one expression; the social milestones are extensions of the literal act. `w527513-s1 + w527513-s2 + w527513-s3` → “여자의 긴 머리를 틀어 얹거나, 기생의 정식 입문이나 여자의 혼인을 이르는 말.”
- **머리(를) 얹히다** — Making a young gisaeng’s hair arrangement and arranging a maiden’s marriage are causative social extensions of setting a woman’s hair. `w527514-s1 + w527514-s2` → “어린 기생을 정식으로 기생이 되게 하거나 처녀를 시집보내다.”
- **머리(를) 올리다** — Arranging long hair, the traditional hair arrangement marking a gisaeng’s formal debut, and a woman’s marriage are related hair-setting uses of one expression; the social milestones are extensions of the literal act. `w527515-s1 + w527515-s2 + w527515-s3` → “여자의 긴 머리를 틀어 올리거나, 기생의 정식 입문이나 여자의 혼인을 이르는 말.”
- **머리공** — The first merit put forward and the greatest merit are two descriptions of the foremost accomplishment. `w112641-s1 + w112641-s2` → “여럿 가운데 맨 먼저 내세우거나 가장 크게 인정할 만한 공.”
- **머리글자** — An initial capital in alphabetic writing is a specific form of the first letter at the beginning of a word, sentence, or proper name. `w420947-s1 + w420947-s2` → “낱말·문장·고유 명사의 첫머리에 나오는 글자, 특히 알파벳 표기에서 쓰는 대문자.”
- **머리끝** — The top/end of the head and the tip of the hair are different physical referents; preserve their separate senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w420925-s1`, `w420925-s2`
- **머리댕기** — A ribbon at the end of braided human hair and a hanging animal tuft shaped like a ribbon are ornamental extensions sharing the same dangling, ribbon-like form. `w112909-s1 + w112909-s2` → “땋은 머리끝에 드리우거나 새·짐승의 머리에 댕기 모양으로 늘어진 헝겊·털.”
- **머리동이** — A kite decorated with a strip of colored paper and a wrap used to bind the head during a headache are different objects despite the shared head-related name. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w112772-s1`, `w112773-s1`
- **머리말** — A preface and the opening part that prepares a discussion are written and spoken forms of the same introductory passage. `w420894-s1 + w420894-s2` → “책·논문이나 말·글에서 본격적인 내용을 앞두고 목적이나 논의의 실마리를 간략히 밝히는 부분.”
- **머리빼기** — The vulgar term for the direction the head faces and the vulgar name for the head itself are distinct directional and body-part uses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w421069-s1`, `w421069-s2`
- **머리뼈안** — The inner space of the skull and the space inside it occupied by the brain identify the same anatomical cavity. `w510612-s1 + w510612-s2` → “머리뼈의 안쪽, 뇌가 차 있는 공간.”
- **머리뼛속** — The inside of the skull and the brain-filled space within it identify the same anatomical cavity. `w535768-s1 + w535768-s2` → “머리뼈의 안쪽에 뇌가 차 있는 공간.”
- **머리소리** — A word-initial phoneme and a high resonant voice produced through the head or upper nasal cavity are distinct phonological and vocal senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w112415-s1`, `w112415-s2`
- **머리시** — An introductory poem hinting at characters and events, a poem replacing a preface, and the preface-like part of a long poem are forms of the same opening poem. `w112420-s1 + w112420-s2 + w112420-s3` → “장편 서사시나 책의 첫머리에서 인물·사건을 암시하거나 머리말 구실을 하는 시.”
- **머리카락에 홈 파겠다** — Being narrow-minded and showing extremely precise workmanship are separate idiomatic evaluations; meticulousness in one does not establish the same meaning as the other. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w527747-s1`, `w527747-s2`
- **머리통** — The circumference of a head and a derogatory name for the head itself are distinct measurement and body-part uses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114426-s1`, `w114426-s2`
- **머리틀** — A wooden hair-setting frame, the head crossbar of an oil press, and the entire oil-press frame are distinct objects or parts; the shared frame imagery does not make them one referent. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114155-s1`, `w114427-s1`, `w427638-s1`
- **머릿결** — Hair quality or condition and the hair itself are related but distinct referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w112541-s1`, `w112541-s2`
- **머릿골** — An oil-press frame and the brain are unrelated referents despite the shared head-related form. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w112542-s1`, `w427234-s1`
- **머릿살** — Nerve strands inside the head and a derogatory term for the head or mind are distinct anatomical and colloquial senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w427408-s1`, `w427408-s2`
- **머릿살(이) 아프다** — A colloquial headache and an unsettled mind are related expressions of being mentally troubled or distressed. `w528069-s1 + w528069-s2` → “(속되게) 머리가 아프거나 마음이 어수선하여 걱정스럽고 괴롭다.”
- **머릿속** — The inner skull cavity, the head’s interior, and the figurative space where thoughts and knowledge reside are concrete and abstract uses of the same inside-the-head concept. `w114736-s1 + w114736-s2 + w114736-s3` → “머리뼈 안쪽의 뇌가 든 공간이나, 생각·상상·지식이 자리한다고 여기는 추상적인 공간.”
- **머릿수** — A count of people, a money amount, and the leading digit of a multi-digit number are distinct counting and positional uses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w420596-s1`, `w420596-s2`, `w420596-s3`
- **머릿수건** — A cloth worn on the head, including winter coverings and hygiene coverings that contain loose hair, is one head-covering item with purpose-specific uses. `w114737-s1 + w114737-s2 + w114737-s3` → “머리에 쓰거나 둘러 감아 추위를 막고 머리카락이 떨어지는 것을 방지하는 수건.”
- **머릿줄** — A long-vowel mark, kite line, flag streamers, and a fishing line are different domain-specific objects; a line shape alone does not establish a shared lexical meaning. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114739-s1`, `w420597-s1`, `w420597-s2`, `w420597-s3`
- **머무르다** — Stopping or staying at a place and failing to progress beyond a level are concrete and figurative uses of remaining in place or state. `w424856-s1 + w424856-s2` → “도중에 멈추거나 어떤 곳에 묵고, 더 나아가지 못해 일정한 수준이나 범위에 그치다.”
- **머물다** — Both shortened-form records correspond to the place-staying and figurative state-staying uses of 머무르다; retain them as one shortened lexical item. `w421121-s1 + w421121-s2` → “‘머무르다’의 준말로, 도중에 멈추거나 묵다 또는 일정한 수준이나 범위에 그치다.”
- **머스크** — Musk-scented plants, the musk deer, and the dried musk-gland perfume are distinct plant, animal, and substance referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w420165-s1`, `w420165-s2`, `w420165-s3`
- **머슴보고 속곳 묻는다** — Asking an unrelated person a question they cannot know and disclosing private matters to a stranger are distinct proverbial criticisms of inappropriate communication. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w527405-s1`, `w527405-s2`
- **머슴아이** — A child who works as a farmhand and a boy disparaged as a male child are distinct occupational and derogatory senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w421102-s1`, `w421102-s2`
- **머슴애** — The two records are shortened forms of the two corresponding 머슴아이 uses; preserve the occupational boy and the derogatory boy together as one shortened lexical item. `w112615-s1 + w112615-s2` → “‘머슴아이’의 준말로, 머슴살이를 하는 아이 또는 남자아이를 낮잡아 이르는 말.”
- **머시** — The spoken placeholder used when a name is forgotten or difficult to say is an extension of the reduced form of 무엇이; both retain the same indefinite 무엇- core. `w112820-s1 + w113886-s1` → “‘무엇이’의 구어적 준말로, 무엇을 가리키거나 이름이 얼른 생각나지 않을 때 대신 쓰는 말.”
- **머쓱하다** — Being awkwardly tall and feeling awkward or embarrassed after being put on the spot share the sense of being out of place. `w113901-s1 + w113901-s2` → “어울리지 않게 키가 크거나, 무안을 당해 어색하고 열없다.”
- **머쓱히** — The adverbial records parallel the awkwardly tall and socially awkward senses of 머쓱하다, which share being out of place. `w420899-s1 + w420899-s2` → “어울리지 않게 키가 크거나 무안을 당해 어색하고 열없이.”
- **머캐덤** — Crushed road aggregate and a road made by compacting that aggregate are the paving material and its resulting surface. `w114352-s1 + w114352-s2` → “도로 포장에 까는 밤자갈이나 그것을 펴서 다져 만든 길.”
- **머큐리** — The chemical element mercury and the English name of the classical god Mercurius are unrelated substance and proper-name senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113947-s1`, `w424844-s1`
- **머플러** — A neck scarf, an engine exhaust silencer, and a boxing glove are distinct objects. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113764-s1`, `w113764-s2`, `w113764-s3`
- **먹** — The solid ink stick and the black liquid made by grinding it are the same ink in solid and prepared forms; the slang term for feces is unrelated. `w114153-s1 + w114153-s2` → “벼루에 갈아 글씨나 그림에 쓰는 검은 먹물, 또는 그것을 굳혀 만든 먹.”; separate senses: `w114154-s1`
- **먹구름** — A very dark cloud extends figuratively to a threatening or unfavorable situation. `w424314-s1 + w424314-s2` → “몹시 검은 구름이나, 좋지 않은 일의 상태를 비유적으로 이르는 말.”
- **먹다** — The ear/nose-blocking record is a separate homonym. Within the eating verb, ingestion, smoking, and inhaling form one intake use; keeping or experiencing emotion, shock, insult, or a blow forms an undergoing/receiving use; age remains an idiomatic extension; bribe, profit, and embezzled property are forms of taking money or benefit; absorption, cutting, coating spread, and infestation are material effects; gaining or losing sports points are scoreboard outcomes; sexual conquest, expenses incurred, and the emphatic auxiliary remain distinct lexicalized uses. `w425031-s1 + w425031-s2 + w425031-s3` → “음식·담배 따위를 먹거나 연기·가스를 들이마시다.”; `w425031-s4 + w425031-s6 + w425031-s7 + w425031-s14` → “감정이나 겁·충격을 마음에 품거나, 욕·매를 듣고 당하다.”; `w425031-s8 + w425031-s9 + w425031-s15` → “뇌물·수익을 챙기거나 맡은 재물을 부당하게 자기 것으로 만들다.”; `w425031-s10 + w425031-s16 + w425031-s17 + w425031-s18` → “물기·바르는 물질이 스며들거나 퍼지고, 벌레·균이 파고들며, 날이 소재를 깎거나 자르다.”; `w425031-s11 + w425031-s12` → “구기 경기에서 점수를 얻거나 잃다.”; separate senses: `w114608-s1`, `w425031-s5`, `w425031-s13`, `w425031-s19`, `w425031-s20`
- **먹딸기** — An overripe dark-red strawberry and the separate plant 까마중 are distinct fruit and species referents despite similar dark color. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114489-s1`, `w114489-s2`
- **먹똥** — Dried ink residue and a mark made by splashed ink are related but distinct material and stain referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114490-s1`, `w114490-s2`
- **먹먹하다** — Muffled hearing and a stuffed, tight feeling in the chest are sensory forms of the same blocked or congested sensation. `w421325-s1 + w421325-s2` → “귀가 막힌 듯 소리가 잘 들리지 않거나 체한 듯 가슴이 답답하다.”
- **먹먹히** — The adverbial records parallel the muffled-hearing and tight-chested uses of 먹먹하다, both describing a blocked sensation. `w114495-s1 + w114495-s2` → “귀가 막힌 듯 잘 들리지 않거나 체한 듯 가슴이 답답하게.”
- **먹물** — Inkstone-ground ink and any ink-black liquid are the same black liquid in a specific and broader description; a learned person or writer is a separate figurative use. `w421304-s1 + w421304-s2` → “벼루에 먹을 갈아 만든 검은 물이나 먹빛처럼 검은 액체.”; separate senses: `w421304-s3`
- **먹보** — A glutton and a covetous, sly person are distinct evaluations of a person and do not share a sufficiently specific core. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113276-s1`, `w420919-s1`
- **먹새** — The manner and quantity of eating are complementary aspects of a person’s eating habits; the dark sand is unrelated. `w112689-s1 + w112689-s2` → “음식을 먹는 태도와 그 분량.”; separate senses: `w420931-s1`
- **먹성** — Food preferences and the amount one eats are complementary aspects of appetite. `w114289-s1 + w114289-s2` → “음식을 좋아하거나 싫어하는 성미와 먹는 분량.”
- **먹수건** — A cloth used to wipe writing from a slate and one used to wipe ink serve the same ink-cleaning purpose. `w114293-s1 + w114293-s2` → “분판의 글씨나 먹물을 닦는 헝겊.”
- **먹승** — A character appearing in traditional mask dance and the mask used for that character are distinct performer and prop referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113246-s1`, `w113246-s2`
- **먹을 콩으로 알고 덤빈다** — Trying to seize something that cannot be eaten extends figuratively to treating someone as easy to exploit or take advantage of. `w527817-s1 + w527817-s2` → “차지하거나 이용할 수 없는 것을 먹을 수 있거나 만만한 것으로 알고 덤비다.”
- **먹을알** — An effortless gain and a gold-rich lode or ore are distinct benefit and geological referents. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113003-s1`, `w113003-s2`

- **먹음새** — The manner of eating and the customary manner of preparing food are related practices but refer to different stages and preserve separate meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113440-s1`, `w113440-s2`
- **먹이다** — The causative feeding and raising of livestock form a nourishment use; making someone experience fear, abuse, a lost point, or a blow is a caused-impact use; giving a bribe remains distinct; making material soak or putting it into a machine is a material-input use. `w421061-s1 + w421061-s9` → “음식을 먹게 하거나 가축을 길러 먹이다.”; `w421061-s2 + w421061-s3 + w421061-s5 + w421061-s6` → “겁·충격을 느끼게 하거나 욕·핀잔·점수를 당하게 하고 매를 맞히다.”; `w421061-s7 + w421061-s8` → “바르는 물질을 배어들게 하거나 기계·틀에 물건이나 원료를 넣다.”; separate senses: `w421061-s4`
- **먹자** — A carpenter’s T-shaped marking tool, a historical servant carrying an ink pot, and a redirect to 목대03 are unrelated tools, roles, and cross-reference senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114102-s1`, `w114103-s1`, `w114104-s1`
- **먹자파** — People who like eating and playing and people who like taking bribes are distinct forms of indulgence and corruption. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w424123-s1`, `w424123-s2`
- **먹자판** — Hedonistic eat-first thinking, a gathering for eating and enjoyment, and a predatory free-for-all are related by excess but denote distinct idea, event, and exploitative situation. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w424124-s1`, `w424124-s2`, `w424124-s3`
- **먹줄** — The inked string used to mark a straight line and the line it leaves are the marking instrument and its result. `w421533-s1 + w421533-s2` → “먹을 묻혀 곧은 줄을 치는 실이나 그것으로 낸 줄.”
- **먹중** — A monk in a black robe, a traditional dance character, and the mask worn for that character are related by costume and performance but remain distinct senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114064-s1`, `w114064-s2`, `w114064-s3`
- **먹지** — A winner in a gambling game and carbon copy paper are unrelated person and material senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114069-s1`, `w421519-s1`
- **먹지 않는 씨아에서 소리만 난다** — A worthless person boasting and someone making noise without doing anything share the idiom’s core of empty talk without substance or action. `w527823-s1 + w527823-s2` → “못난 사람이 실속 없이 큰소리치거나 아무 일도 하지 않고 하는 체하며 떠들다.”
- **먹칠** — Applying black ink, black coating, and the figurative tarnishing of honor are concrete and figurative uses of covering or blackening. `w115786-s1 + w115786-s2 + w115786-s3` → “먹이나 먹처럼 검은 것으로 칠하거나, 비유적으로 명예·체면을 더럽히는 일.”
- **먹칠하다** — Applying ink and figuratively tarnishing honor or face are literal and figurative uses of blackening or covering. `w421700-s1 + w421700-s2` → “먹으로 칠하거나, 비유적으로 명예·체면을 더럽히다.”
- **먹탈** — A character in the Tongyeong Ogwangdae and the black-based mask used for that character are distinct performer and prop senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w115258-s1`, `w115258-s2`
- **먹통** — An obtuse person and a malfunctioning object or service share the figurative sense of being blocked or not functioning; the two physical ink containers are distinct tool types but share an ink-holding line-making purpose. `w115259-s1 + w115259-s2` → “사리에 어둡거나 제대로 작동하지 않는 사람·물건·서비스를 낮잡아 이르는 말.”; `w115315-s1 + w115315-s2` → “먹물을 담거나 먹줄을 치는 데 쓰는 통.”
- **먹혀들다** — Being understood or accepted and being seized or taken over are distinct reception and dispossession meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w114992-s1`, `w114993-s1`
- **먹히다** — Food being eaten and resources being spent are consumption uses; food being easy to eat is a receptive-eating variant; words or actions being accepted and a sight or sound being masked are distinct figurative extensions. `w421493-s1 + w421493-s4 + w421493-s5` → “음식이나 돈·물자 따위가 소비되거나, 거부감 없이 잘 먹히다.”; separate senses: `w421493-s2`, `w421493-s3`
- **먼가랫밥** — Soil lifted and thrown with a spade and the soil used for temporary burial of a traveler are the same excavated soil with a specific burial use. `w113725-s1 + w421495-s1` → “가래로 떠서 던지거나 객사한 사람을 임시로 묻는 데 쓰는 흙.”
- **먼눈** — An eye that cannot see due to loss of sight and an eye gazing into the distance are distinct visual and directional senses. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w113931-s1`, `w113932-s1`

- **무뎌지다** — All three abbreviated senses retain the same verb identity of becoming dull, whether an edge, a capacity for feeling or expression, or a skill loses keenness. `w515398-s1 + w515398-s2 + w515398-s3` → “날카로운 끝이나 느끼고 깨닫고 표현하는 힘, 또는 솜씨가 둔해지다.”
- **무도** — Immoral conduct is an independent homonym. The dance senses describe dancing as an act, an art, and a dance movement; the martial-art and warrior-virtue senses share the martial-way identity. `w119679-s1 + w119679-s2 + w119679-s3` → “음악에 맞추어 몸을 움직여 춤추는 일이나 예술, 또는 그 춤사위.”; `w120504-s1 + w120504-s2` → “무예와 무술, 또는 무사가 지녀야 할 품격과 도리.”; separate senses: `w119677-s1`
- **무도장** — A place for martial-arts practice or contests and a room for social dancing serve different activities and are separate lexical identities of 무도. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w119618-s1`, `w119619-s1`
- **무도하다** — The verb meaning to dance and the adjective meaning to act without moral principle are independent homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w119680-s1`, `w426051-s1`
- **무도회장** — A ballroom and the euphemistic use for a nightclub are venue and softened naming uses of the same social-dance place. `w518441-s1 + w518441-s2` → “여러 사람이 함께 춤추며 사교를 하는 장소, 또는 이를 완곡하게 이르는 나이트클럽.”
- **무독하다** — Having no poisonous or harmful quality extends to describing a person as harmless, gentle, and kind. `w423254-s1 + w423254-s2` → “독하거나 해로운 것이 없고, 사람의 성품이 순하고 착하다.”
- **무동** — The meditative state is an independent homonym. The court performer, folk troupe acrobat, and Bukcheong lion-play character are regional and historical roles of the same child performer identity. `w119842-s1 + w119842-s2 + w119842-s3` → “궁중 잔치나 농악·걸립패, 북청 사자놀음에서 춤과 재주를 보이는 아이.”; separate senses: `w119841-s1`
- **무두** — The mountain gatherers' slang for a tree and the adjective-like sense 'without a head' are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w119903-s1`, `w423441-s1`
- **무두질** — Tanning and working material shares a lexical image with the figurative description of a painful, grinding hunger or stomachache. `w119595-s1 + w119595-s2` → “생가죽이나 실을 매만져 부드럽게 만드는 일, 또는 배가 몹시 고프거나 속이 쓰리고 아픈 상태를 비유적으로 이르는 말.”
- **무두질하다** — Working hide or yarn, describing a painful grinding hunger, and colloquially twisting someone in torture extend the same rough working or twisting action. `w423245-s1 + w423245-s2 + w423245-s3` → “생가죽이나 실을 매만져 부드럽게 하거나, 비유적으로 배가 몹시 고프고 속이 쓰리게 하며, 속되게 사람에게 주리를 틀다.”
- **무드** — The everyday emotional atmosphere and the technical logical and grammatical uses share the borrowed concept of a mood or mode: a state of feeling or a formal type in which reasoning or a sentence is expressed. `w422387-s1 + w422387-s2 + w422387-s3` → “상황의 분위기나 기분, 또는 논증·문장의 형식이나 유형을 나타내는 말.”
- **무등** — Lacking rank or distinction naturally extends to being without equal and therefore unsurpassed. `w118259-s1 + w118259-s2` → “등급이나 차별이 없고, 그 이상 더할 수 없을 만큼 뛰어나다.”
- **무등산** — The historical song takes its name and subject from the mountain; the place name and the song titled for that place are kept in one named-identity group. `w422957-s1 + w422957-s2` → “전남 지역의 산 이름이며, 그 산에 성을 쌓은 일을 기뻐해 백제가 불렀다는 가요의 제목이기도 하다.”
- **무등하다** — Having no rank or distinction extends to having no equal and being unsurpassable in degree. `w118260-s1 + w118260-s2` → “등급이나 차별이 없고, 정도가 그 이상 더할 수 없다.”
- **무디다** — A blunt edge, dull perception or expression, and an unpolished rough quality are physical, mental, and aesthetic extensions of lacking sharpness. `w423172-s1 + w423172-s2 + w423172-s3` → “날카롭지 않고 둔하며, 감각·표현이나 세련된 맛이 부족해 투박하다.”
- **무디어지다** — An edge, perception or expression, and a skill becoming dull are extensions of the same loss of keenness. `w515400-s1 + w515400-s2 + w515400-s3` → “날카로운 끝이나 감각·표현력, 또는 솜씨가 점차 둔해지다.”
- **무뚝무뚝** — The eating and speaking uses share a segmented, chunky rhythm: large bites or deliberate, spaced articulation. `w422975-s1 + w422975-s2` → “덩어리 음식을 큼직하게 베어 먹거나 말을 간격을 두어 또박또박 하는 모양.”
- **무뚝무뚝하다** — The verb senses parallel the same segmented, chunky rhythm in eating and deliberate, spaced speech. `w117953-s1 + w117953-s2` → “덩어리 음식을 큼직하게 베어 먹거나 말을 간격을 두어 또박또박 하다.”
- **무뜩** — A thought suddenly occurring and an action happening suddenly are the same suddenness adverb across mental and eventive contexts. `w117956-s1 + w117956-s2` → “생각이나 느낌이 갑자기 떠오르거나 어떤 행위가 갑자기 이루어지는 모양.”
- **무랍** — Tax collected by public authorities and ritual rice cast as an offering to spirits are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w121196-s1`, `w121618-s1`
- **무랑** — The ritual-music performer and the official post held by a military-origin clerk designate different historical roles, with no source-bound semantic extension connecting them. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w121197-s1`, `w121197-s2`
- **무량** — An immeasurably large amount and the theological attribute of God's infinity share the core concept of being without limit. `w121201-s1 + w121201-s2` → “헤아릴 수 없을 만큼 많거나 한계가 없는 상태.”
- **무량대수** — The senses name the same extremely large number; their numerical value differs between the current and historical counting systems, not in lexical identity. `w120922-s1 + w120922-s2 + w120922-s3` → “불가사의보다 큰 수인 무량대수를 이르며, 현재는 10⁶⁸, 옛 수 체계에서는 10¹²⁸을 가리켰다.”
- **무량수** — The immeasurably long lifespan and the Buddhist reference to Amitabha and the beings of his land share one infinite-lifespan sense. The homonymous large-number entry remains separate; its senses are historical/current values of the same named number. `w121077-s1 + w121077-s2` → “헤아릴 수 없이 오랜 수명, 특히 아미타불과 그 땅의 백성의 한량없는 수명.”; `w121078-s1 + w121078-s2 + w121078-s3` → “불가사의보다 큰 수인 무량수를 이르며, 현재는 10⁶⁸, 옛 수 체계에서는 10¹²⁸을 가리켰다.”
- **무럭무럭** — The image of something steadily swelling or rising extends from vigorous growth to smoke, scent, vapor, thoughts, and feelings arising in abundance. `w426880-s1 + w426880-s2 + w426880-s3` → “식물이 힘차게 자라거나 연기·냄새·김, 느낌·생각 따위가 계속 많이 피어오르는 모양.”
- **무럼생선** — The food name for jellyfish extends figuratively to a weak-looking or spineless person, drawing on the same soft, limp image. `w121376-s1 + w121376-s2 + w121376-s3` → “식품으로 이르는 해파리, 또는 그 연하고 힘없는 모습을 빗대어 허약하거나 줏대 없는 사람을 놀림조로 이르는 말.”
- **무려** — The military force and the adverb emphasizing an unexpectedly large number are independent homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w120277-s1`, `w120278-s1`
- **무력** — Lack of strength and exerting effort are separate homonyms. The military force and bodily force senses are domains of the same use of force. The valid cross-reference to 물력 remains bound to its own source sense. `w426466-s1 + w426466-s2` → “군사적으로 쓰는 힘이나 때리거나 부수는 데 쓰는 육체적 힘.”; separate senses: `w120508-s1`, `w120510-s1`, `w426294-s1`
- **무력전** — A felt piece attached beneath a bow part and a war fought using force are unrelated homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w121677-s1`, `w121678-s1`
- **무력하다** — Having no strength and exerting strength are independent homonyms with opposite meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w120509-s1`, `w427061-s1`
- **무렴** — Lack of shame and the discomfort felt on recognizing one's shamelessness are linked uses of the same concept of propriety and shame. `w122266-s1 + w122266-s2` → “염치가 없거나, 염치없는 자신을 부끄럽고 거북하게 여기는 상태.”
- **무렴하다** — Being shameless and feeling discomfort at one's lack of shame are related uses of the same propriety concept. `w427213-s1 + w427213-s2` → “염치가 없거나, 염치없는 자신을 부끄럽고 거북하게 여기다.”
- **무렴히** — The adverbial senses parallel the adjective's linked lack-of-shame and discomfort uses. `w122267-s1 + w122267-s2` → “염치없이 하거나 염치없음을 부끄럽고 거북하게 여기는 모양.”
- **무로이** — Unusually heavy or persistent fog and unusually abundant dew are related manifestations of excessive atmospheric moisture, not independent lexical identities. `w122097-s1 + w122097-s2` → “안개가 아주 짙거나 오래 끼고, 이슬이 이상하게 많이 내리는 현상.”
- **무론** — The phrase 'needless to say' appears as a noun-like expression and an adverb; the grammatical form does not create a second lexical identity. `w426625-s1 + w426625-s2` → “말할 것도 없거나 말할 것도 없이.”
- **무롱** — Abusing written language extends to tampering with documents, accounts, or rules; playing a string instrument is a separate homonym. `w122983-s1 + w122983-s2` → “붓글이나 문서·장부를 함부로 다루거나 법규의 적용을 농락함.”; separate senses: `w122985-s1`
- **무롱하다** — The verb senses for manipulating writing and tampering with documents or rules are the same abuse-of-writing use; playing an instrument is a separate homonym. `w122984-s1 + w122984-s2` → “붓글이나 문서·장부를 함부로 다루거나 법규의 적용을 농락하다.”; separate senses: `w426626-s1`
- **무료** — Absence of a fee or salary is one no-compensation identity. Being bored and feeling awkward or embarrassed are related emotional uses of the other homonym. `w122036-s1 + w122036-s2` → “요금이나 급료가 없음.”; `w122087-s1 + w122087-s2` → “흥미가 없어 심심하고 지루하거나 부끄럽고 열없음.”
- **무료하다** — Boredom and awkward embarrassment are related emotional uses of 무료하다, not separate words. `w426798-s1 + w426798-s2` → “흥미가 없어 심심하고 지루하거나 부끄럽고 열없다.”
- **무료히** — The adverbial boredom and awkwardness senses parallel one related emotional identity. `w122088-s1 + w122088-s2` → “흥미가 없어 심심하고 지루하거나 부끄럽고 열없이.”
- **무루** — The gatherers' slang for a tree, the Buddhist state free from afflictions, and the historical monk's personal name are independent identities. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w122277-s1`, `w122279-s1`, `w424246-s1`
- **무루지** — An uncontaminated Buddhist realm and the pure wisdom free from afflictions are descriptions of the same no-outflow wisdom concept. `w122338-s1 + w424258-s1` → “번뇌에 물들지 않은 깨끗한 경계이자 모든 번뇌를 떠난 청정한 지혜.”
- **무류** — Absence of error and being peerless are independent homonyms with distinct source meanings. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w122990-s1`, `w122992-s1`
- **무류하다** — Being without error and being peerless are independent homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w122991-s1`, `w424032-s1`
- **무르녹다** — Fruit or food becoming fully ripe and an event or state reaching its height are literal and figurative extensions of ripeness. `w424034-s1 + w424034-s2` → “과일이나 음식이 충분히 익어 흐무러지거나, 일이나 상태가 한창 무르익다.”
- **무르다** — Softness extends from physical material and excess moisture to a tender or weak disposition and slack work. The senses of returning a purchase, reversing an action, and moving backward form a separate return/reversal verb identity. `w121874-s1 + w121874-s2 + w121874-s3 + w121874-s4 + w122518-s1` → “단단하지 않고 여리거나 물기가 많아 물렁거리며, 마음·힘·일 처리나 솜씨도 야무지지 못하다.”; `w424047-s1 + w424047-s2 + w424047-s3` → “산 물건이나 이미 한 일을 이전 상태로 돌리거나, 있던 자리에서 뒤로 옮기다.”
- **무르와내다** — Lifting something away from an elder and receiving something from an elder are honorific ways of taking or receiving from a superior. `w424025-s1 + w424025-s2` → “윗사람 앞에 놓인 것을 들어 내오거나 윗사람에게서 무엇을 받다.”
- **무르익다** — Ripening crops and an event or time becoming mature are literal and figurative extensions of maturity. `w424026-s1 + w424026-s2` → “과일이나 곡식이 충분히 익거나 시기·일이 충분히 성숙하다.”
- **무른 땅에 말뚝 박기** — The easy-task sense and the oppression-of-the-weak sense share the idiom's image of an easy act against something yielding or powerless. `w521445-s1 + w521445-s2` → “무른 땅에 말뚝을 박듯 하기 쉬운 일을 하거나, 힘 있는 사람이 힘없고 연약한 사람을 업신여기고 학대함을 비유적으로 이르는 말.”
- **무름병** — Silkworm rot and plant soft rot are organism-specific instances of the same disease concept: tissue softening and rotting. `w121351-s1 + w121351-s2` → “누에나 즙이 많은 식물의 조직이 물렁해지고 썩어 문드러지는 병.”
- **무릅쓰다** — Putting something over the head is the concrete base that extends figuratively to bearing difficulty, danger, or other adversity. `w424003-s1 + w424003-s2` → “무엇을 뒤집어 머리에 덮어 쓰거나 힘들고 어려운 일을 참고 견디다.”
- **무릇** — The plant and its dried bulb are the same plant identity; the generalizing adverb is an independent homonym. `w121406-s1 + w121406-s2` → “백합과의 여러해살이풀인 무릇, 또는 그 비늘줄기를 말린 것.”; separate senses: `w423775-s1`
- **무릎도가니** — The beef knee joint and the vulgar name for the kneecap refer to the same knee-bone area and its attached meat. `w121917-s1 + w121917-s2` → “소 무릎의 무릎뼈와 거기에 붙은 고깃덩이, 또는 무릎뼈를 속되게 이르는 말.”
- **무릎쏴** — The kneeling firing command and the movement performed in response to it are command and action uses of the same drill term. `w427806-s1 + w427806-s2` → “한쪽 무릎을 꿇고 다른 무릎은 세워 사격하라는 구령이나 그 구령에 따라 하는 동작.”
- **무릎앉아** — The command to sit with one knee down and the posture performed in response are command and action uses of the same drill term. `w122248-s1 + w122248-s2` → “오른쪽 무릎은 땅에 대고 왼쪽 무릎은 세워 앉으라는 구령이나 그 구령에 따라 하는 동작.”
- **무릎치기** — Both garments are named for reaching the knee: short pants ending at the knee and a traditional top whose collar reaches the knee are clothing variants under the same knee-length identity. `w427808-s1 + w427808-s2` → “무릎까지 내려오거나 깃이 무릎께에 닿는 옛 옷.”
- **무리** — People, animals, and objects gathered together extend to a group of workers emerging together or products arriving in a seasonal batch. Rice sediment and the paste made from its flour form one food-starch identity. The sun or moon halo is a separate optical sense. General excess or irrationality extends to the mathematical irrational-number use. `w122484-s1 + w427614-s1 + w427614-s2` → “사람·짐승·사물이 모인 한 동아리나, 사람이 함께 나오고 생산물이 한꺼번에 많이 나오는 무리.”; `w122539-s1 + w122539-s2` → “물에 불린 쌀을 갈아 가라앉힌 앙금이나, 그 가루로 쑨 풀.”; `w122937-s1 + w122937-s2` → “도리나 이치에 맞지 않거나 정도에서 지나치게 벗어남. 수학에서는 유리 연산 이외의 관계를 포함하는 수의 성질을 이른다.”; separate senses: `w122933-s1`
- **무리개** — A contemptuous reference to an underling from 무리 and a woman who acts as a sexual go-between designate independent identities. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w123898-s1`, `w123898-s2`
- **무리난제** — An unreasonably difficult problem and an unreasonable quarrel are applications of the same unjustified, excessive demand or dispute. `w123902-s1 + w123902-s2` → “무리하게 떠맡기는 어렵고 까다로운 문제나 터무니없는 시비.”
- **무리미** — Rice as the gatherers' slang and rice offered at a mountain-spirit rite are food and ritual-use extensions of the same food identity. `w122078-s1 + w122078-s2` → “심마니들의 은어로 밥, 특히 산신제에 올리는 메를 이르는 말.”
- **무리수** — The mathematical irrational number is an independent homonym. A greedy move in Go extends to a figuratively unreasonable or excessive method. `w519017-s1 + w519017-s2` → “바둑에서 과욕을 부려 두는 수, 또는 도리와 이치에 맞지 않게 지나친 방식.”; separate senses: `w121415-s1`
- **무리하다** — Being contrary to reason and exceeding a proper degree are two descriptions of the same unreasonable excess. `w430292-s1 + w430292-s2` → “도리나 이치에 맞지 않거나 정도에서 지나치게 벗어나다.”
- **무림** — Gatherers' slang for rice, the martial-arts world, a dense forest, and the verb for ruling and caring for people are independent homonyms. no merges; preserve each current Meaning; separate senses: `w120696-s1`, `w120697-s1`, `w120698-s1`, `w120751-s1`
- **무릿매** — The sling that hurls a stone extends metaphorically to a barrage of beating by many people, preserving the image of a forceful volley. `w423829-s1 + w423829-s2` → “끈에 맨 작은 돌을 휘둘러 던지는 팔매, 또는 여러 사람이 한꺼번에 덤벼 때리는 매.”
- **무릿매질** — Throwing stones with a sling and raining blows on someone extend the same repeated, forceful attack image. `w121420-s1 + w121420-s2` → “무릿매로 돌을 던지거나 사정없이 몰아치는 매질.”
- **무릿매질하다** — Throwing stones with a sling and delivering repeated blows are actions extending the same forceful attack image. `w121421-s1 + w121421-s2` → “무릿매로 돌을 던지거나 사정없이 몰아쳐 매질하다.”
- **무마** — Training a horse to perform tricks is a separate homonym. Rubbing with the hand extends figuratively to soothing feelings and smoothing over a dispute or incident. `w423830-s1 + w423830-s2 + w423830-s3` → “손으로 두루 어루만지거나, 타이르고 얼러 마음을 달래며, 분쟁이나 사건을 어물어물 덮어 버림.”; separate senses: `w121422-s1`
